```text
1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related-work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Self-Consistency and extensions.}
5	SC selects the most frequent answer across chain-of-thought traces \citep{wang2023sc}. Extensions include universal SC for free-form generation \citep{chen2024usc}, confidence weighting \citep{taubenfeld2025cisc}, diverse prompting with step-aware verification \citep{li2023diverse}, program-based vote filtering \citep{toh2024notallvotes}, and reasoning-aware filtering \citep{wan2024rasc, kim2026reasc}. \citet{loo2025reevaluating} show that SC gains plateau early; \citet{byerly2026selfconsistency} further analyze SC's limitations in mathematical reasoning. Our Condorcet analysis explains this: $\kappa = 0.21$--$0.82$ compresses $K_{\text{eff}}$ to 1.20--3.62 for $K = 12$ (\S\ref{sec:effective-k}).
6	
7	\paragraph{Neuro-symbolic reasoning.}
8	We distinguish three paradigms. \emph{Specification-generation} (Logic-LM \citep{pan2023logiclm}, LINC \citep{olausson2023linc}, SatLM \citep{ye2024satlm}) translates problem descriptions into formal specifications before any answer is generated; LINC documents ${\sim}$40\% translation errors even at this level. \emph{Program-generation} \citep{gao2023pal,chen2023program,lyu2023faithful} produces executable code from problem statements. Both paradigms formalize \emph{before} answer generation. \emph{Trace-extraction} (this work) operates post-hoc on traces that already contain candidates, inheriting confirmation bias (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Recent extensions \citep{ryu2025clover, xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, singh2026verge, luo2025logicagent, miya2025eidoku} introduce capabilities beyond the reasoning model. \citet{amonkar2025realitycheck} show formalization degrades with complexity; \citet{chan2026position} argue logical soundness is unreliable for neurosymbolic verification. Our oracle controls confirm the mechanism is sound ($+29.90$/$+13.73$\,pp FOLIO, $+17.17$\,pp ProofWriter); the failure localizes to self-extraction (Appendix~\ref{app:oracle}).
9	
10	\paragraph{Test-time compute and verification.}
11	Scaling test-time computation through additional traces \citep{snell2025scaling} or structured search \citep{yao2023tree} faces the same correlation challenge: same-model attempts collapse into correlated solutions. Reasoning models (o1, DeepSeek-R1 \citep{guo2025deepseekr1}, QwQ \citep{qwen2024qwq}) extend thinking time but remain subject to correlated same-model voting: QwQ-32B produces $\kappa = 0.91$ and $C_0 = 2.6\%$, suggesting that longer reasoning chains intensify homogenization rather than alleviating it. Trained verifiers offer an alternative \citep{cobbe2021gsm8k, lightman2024prm, wang2024mathshepherd}; multi-agent debate refines answers through discussion \citep{liang2023encouraging, du2023improving}. Self-verification \citep{weng2023selfverification}, chain-of-verification \citep{dhuliawala2023chainofverification}, self-refinement \citep{madaan2023selfrefine}, tool-augmented critique \citep{gou2024critic}, and formal CoT verification \citep{zhu2025vericot} report gains, but none systematically tests self-extracted constraints under formal solvers. Recent self-verification methods in test-time scaling \citep{chen2025sets} show positive results using trained verification signals, contrasting with our negative findings for untrained symbolic extraction; multi-agent verification approaches \citep{lifshitz2025mav} align with our cross-architecture evidence that structurally independent verifiers can escape SCB (\S\ref{sec:cross-arch}). We find the bottleneck shifts from the verifier to the extractor: self-extracted constraints inherit the model's bias regardless of verification formalism (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Our diagnostics (BR, $K_\text{eff}$, process-$\kappa$) predict when trace-aggregation will fail.
12	
13	\paragraph{CJT and independence.}
14	CJT \citep{condorcet1785} guarantees convergence under independent voting; \citet{ladha1992correlated} show correlated voters reduce effective ensemble size. Our $K_\text{eff}$ adapts Fleiss' $\kappa$ from ensemble diversity theory \citep{kuncheva2003measures, brown2005diversity}. \citet{kim2025correlated} demonstrate correlated errors in LLM outputs; \citet{kuai2026independent} propose BEI and CIG for cross-model independence auditing, complementary to our within-model process-$\kappa$. SCB is distinct from CoT unfaithfulness \citep{turpin2024language} and CoT-level confirmation bias \citep{wan2025unveiling, jhaveri2026failing}: the latter concern whether reasoning reflects actual computation, while SCB concerns whether \emph{self-extracted formal constraints} mirror the model's answer distribution.
15	
16	\paragraph{Positioning.}
17	\looseness=-1 \citet{huang2024selfcorrect} show LLMs cannot self-correct without external feedback---a general observation about natural-language self-correction. We provide a specific, quantified diagnosis in the formal verification setting: provably sound Z3 verification adds no information beyond majority voting under self-extraction (BR $1.66$--$4.97\times$ across nine models), a \emph{symbolic mirage} not addressed by prior frameworks. Neuro-symbolic methods with positive results \citep{ye2024satlm, xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover} succeed because they use specification-generation, trained extractors, or curated knowledge; our finding explains \emph{why} self-extraction fails---constraints inherit the model's distributional prior. Prior formalization studies note high error rates \citep{olausson2023linc, amonkar2025realitycheck} but treat them as quality problems; we show the errors are systematically biased toward the model's answer (shuffle control: $\text{BR}_\text{null} \approx \text{BR}_\text{obs}$, $p > 0.7$), a distributional bias distinct from random translation errors.
```

```text
1	\looseness=-1 Open-source models range from 7B to 32B parameters with self-extraction; frontier models confirm SCB universality but use cross-model extraction via Mistral-7B, as closed-source APIs do not expose internals---whether self-extraction on frontier models would yield different BR values remains untested. All datasets are English-language logical reasoning benchmarks; we do not evaluate trained symbolic extractors, multilingual settings, or commonsense domains lacking gold annotations. NLI verification was tested only on propositional/first-order logic tasks; domain-adapted NLI amplifies gains dramatically but ProofWriter's synthetic templates contribute to the high verifier accuracy; generalization to naturalistic benchmarks and non-logical reasoning domains remains untested. Syntactic deduplication (46.0\% removal rate) may retain semantically equivalent constraints; however, the shuffle control (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}) preserves the same duplication pattern and yields $\text{BR}_\text{shuffle} \approx \text{BR}_\text{obs}$, suggesting deduplication granularity does not drive the observed bias direction. McNemar power is below 21\% for $|\Delta| = 2$\,pp at $n{=}200$--$600$, meaning small true effects may go undetected; most conditions use a single seed; multi-seed replication on Mistral-7B (3 seeds) and Qwen2.5-14B (4 seeds) FOLIO confirms the single-seed patterns ($\Delta$ range spans ${\sim}6$\,pp and ${\sim}2$\,pp, respectively; Table~\ref{tab:multi-seed}), with multi-seed data for remaining conditions in progress. The capability-gap analysis rests on $n{=}6$ conditions sharing verifier components ($p = 0.167$ after collapsing); we characterize it as a consistent pattern rather than a confirmed law. SICA requires $4.5\times$ the wall-clock time of SC with no significant accuracy gain. Extended limitations are discussed in Appendix~\ref{app:limitations-extended}.
2	
```

1	\section{Regime Scatter Plot}
2	\label{app:regime-scatter}
3	
4	\begin{figure}[t]
5	\centering
6	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{f2_regime_scatter}
7	\caption{SICA $\Delta$ (pp) vs.\ SC baseline. Filled: $n \geq 100$; open: $n < 100$. The intermediate-SC region (50--60\%, shaded) contains the only positive trends (none reaching $p < 0.05$). Type~C: nominally significant degradation (raw $p = 0.014$; BH adj.\ $p = 0.112$).}
8	\label{fig:regime-scatter}
9	\end{figure}
10	
11	\section{Prompt Templates}
12	\label{app:prompts}
13	
14	SICA uses domain-specific prompt templates for constraint extraction: one for mathematical reasoning (GSM8K, AIME) and one for logical reasoning (ProofWriter, FOLIO). Both share the same system prompt. The LLM is called with temperature $T{=}0.3$, max tokens $1024$, and structured JSON output mode. Up to 6 traces are processed in parallel via thread pooling, with 2 retries on failure (exponential backoff, base 0.5s).
15	
16	\paragraph{System Prompt.}
17	
18	\begin{lstlisting}
19	You are a constraint extraction tool. Output ONLY
20	valid JSON. Do NOT solve problems, reason about
21	correctness, or think step-by-step. Extract
22	constraints from the given trace exactly as stated.
23	\end{lstlisting}
24	
25	\paragraph{Mathematical Constraint Extraction (EXTRACTION\_PROMPT).}
26	
27	Used for GSM8K and AIME datasets. The placeholder \texttt{\{trace\}} is replaced with the reasoning trace at runtime.
28	
29	\begin{lstlisting}
30	Extract mathematical constraints from this reasoning
31	trace as a JSON object.
32	
33	Rules:
34	- type: "equation", "inequality", or "predicate"
35	- expression: human-readable, e.g. "x + y == 14"
36	- z3_formula: a single comparison expression in
37	  Python Z3 syntax
38	- source_step: step number where the constraint
39	  appears
40	- Use variable names from the trace
41	
42	z3_formula MUST follow these rules:
43	- Allowed operators: ==, !=, <, >, <=, >=, +, -, *,
44	  /, **, %
45	- Use individual named variables: a1, a2, a3...
46	  NOT arrays a[1] or sets A
47	- Expand all sums explicitly:
48	  "a1 + a2 + a3 == 55" NOT "sum([a1,a2,a3])"
49	- NO Python builtins: sum(), len(), max(), min(),
50	  list(), set(), range(), int()
51	- Each z3_formula must be exactly ONE comparison
52	  (==, !=, <, >, <=, >=)
53	- For multiple values, write separate constraints
54	  for each
55	
56	Output ONLY the JSON below, nothing else:
57	{"constraints": [{"type": "equation",
58	  "expression": "x + y == 14",
59	  "z3_formula": "x + y == 14",
60	  "source_step": 1}],
61	 "answer": "final_answer",
62	 "variables": ["x", "y"]}
63	
64	Trace:
65	{trace}
66	\end{lstlisting}
67	
68	\paragraph{Logical Constraint Extraction (LOGIC\_EXTRACTION\_PROMPT).}
69	
70	Used for ProofWriter and FOLIO datasets.
71	
72	\begin{lstlisting}
73	IMPORTANT: You are an EXTRACTION tool, NOT a problem
74	solver.
75	- Do NOT re-solve or re-reason about the problem.
76	- Do NOT verify whether the trace's reasoning is
77	  correct.
78	- ONLY extract the logical constraints that are
79	  explicitly stated or used in the trace below.
80	- Output ONLY the JSON object, nothing else.
```

I'm deciding to remove those empirical finding boxes entirely and integrate them as regular paragraphs in section 5 instead—they'll feel less like proven theorems that way. Given the deadline of 5/25 and that it's already 5/23, I need to work through a tight two-day schedule: tackle the method, results, and symbolic mirage sections today, then finish the remaining sections, figures, and consistency checks tomorrow before final submission.

Now I'm mapping out the actual section structure. The current paper has introduction, related work, background and setup, experiments with the main results table, analysis covering effective-K and SCB, and cross-architecture sections. I'm consolidating this into a cleaner flow: introduction, related work, merged method and setup, results with the regime analysis, the symbolic mirage analysis, escaping the bottleneck for cross-architecture findings, and discussion.

The key moves are pulling experimental setup into the methods section, promoting regime analysis into results, consolidating the symbolic mirage concept into its own section with diagnostics and the hypothesis, moving the process-kappa figure to appendix, and significantly expanding the discussion. This is a substantial reorganization that should improve the narrative flow.

Now I'm thinking through what the abstract needs to do differently. The current version leads with the saturation problem, but the new framing should open with the fundamental tension: can formal verification actually help LLM reasoning when the model extracts its own constraints? I want to add a caveat about power limitations and dial back the cross-architecture generalization, while keeping the diagnostic findings intact.

The key results I need to capture are stark: the formal layer adds nothing on most problems, self-extracted constraints are systematically biased toward wrong answers even when they pass validation, and attempts to fix this fail across the board. But the oracle experiments show the approach itself is sound—the bottleneck is the extraction, not the verification.

For the next part, I'm noting that self-extraction only captures about 12% of the theoretical ceiling, though I should include the caveat that ten of the sixteen conditions tested are underpowered. The zero-cost diagnostics are promising for predicting when aggregation will work, and there's some initial evidence from cross-architecture NLI verification that we might escape this constraint, though I should be more cautious about that claim.

I'm also reconsidering the title—the current one focuses on self-consistency, but "The Symbolic Mirage" better captures the core finding that formal verification creates an illusion of correctness without actually improving reasoning. I think that's the stronger framing, so I'll go with that and prepare the YAML for submission.

Before I finalize the plan, I'm catching some data inconsistencies: the solutions.tex file still has outdated numbers (39.33/+6.67) that should reflect the corrected values (39.17/+7.00) from the latest update, and introduction.tex has the same stale "+6.67pp" figure on line 28. I'll need to flag these discrepancies in my plan so they get corrected during the restructuring.

2	name: paper-quality-lessons
3	description: NeurIPS 投稿实战经验总结——人工审查中提炼的写作规范、审查清单、常见反模式。Paper Agent / Reviewer / Director 写论文或审查论文时加载。

18	---
19	
20	# 论文写作质量指南（NeurIPS 实战经验）
21	
22	> 来源：2026 年 NeurIPS 投稿期间，从 20+ 项目的人工审查反馈中提炼。
23	> 每条规则都源自真实的 bug / 退回 / 审稿质疑。
24	
25	---
26	
27	## 一、数据诚信与溯源（最高优先级）
28	
29	### 1.1 数字必须有源头
30	
31	- 论文中**每一个数字**必须可追溯到 registry 实验 ID 或原始结果文件
32	- 禁止凭记忆写数字——必须 `cat result.json | head` + `ls -la` 附上文件证据
33	- 计算值（均值、标准差、DID、delta）必须独立重算，不信 Agent 第一手汇报
34	- `paper_provenance.yaml` 覆盖率必须 100%，改数字必须同步更新 provenance
35	
36	> **教训（D149 事件）**：Agent 曾伪造完整的 metrics 数据包括 p-value，事后被 Reviewer 独立 parse JSON 抓出。从此所有关键数据必须 Reviewer 独立验证到字段级别。
37	
38	### 1.2 不同来源数据不能混用
39	
40	- 配对数据必须同 seed、同 c 值、同 N、同 evaluation protocol
41	- 两张表如果 protocol 不同（如 0-shot vs 8-shot），论文必须明确说明
42	- 跨 config 比较 delta 会引入系统误差——必须补跑同 config baseline 后才能比较
43	- 非等量对比必须显式标注（如 "at unmatched volumes"）
44	
45	> **教训（D027 事件）**：不同 config 文件的 baseline 不同，跨 config 比较 delta 值引入了 artifact。
46	
47	### 1.3 结论撤回后不可复活
48	
49	- 被 Director shelved 的数据（如因截断混淆），禁止重新引入论文
50	- `paper_provenance.yaml` 中 `verified: false` 的 claim，不得恢复
51	- 术语修正后不可回退（如 "same norm" → "same per-layer standard deviation"）
52	
53	---
54	
55	## 二、Claim 强度与数据匹配
56	
57	### 2.1 Claim 强度规则
58	
59	| 数据支撑 | 允许的最强表述 |
60	|----------|--------------|
61	| 单 seed, 单数据集 | "preliminary observation", "initial evidence" |
62	| 单 seed, 多数据集 | "suggestive pattern", "indicative trend" |
63	| 多 seed, 单数据集 | 报告均值±标准差，可写 "we find" |
64	| 多 seed, 多数据集 | 可用 "robust", "consistent" |
65	| 统计检验显著 | 可用 "significant", 附 p-value |
66	| 单 seed + 外推到其他条件 | 必须加限定，不能声称 "scale-invariant" 等 |
67	
68	### 2.2 禁止的过强表述
69	
70	- "remarkably stable" → 改为 "relatively stable" 或给出量化波动范围
71	- "confirmed" → 仅 "suggestive" 级别数据不能用
72	- "scale-invariant" → single-seed 验证不够
73	- "We attribute X to Y" → 如果是假说，必须改为 "We hypothesize" + "requiring causal verification"
74	- "roughly twice" → 实际比值必须在 1.8x-2.2x 才能用，否则改为更准确的近似
75	- "domain-agnostic" → 改为 "robust across N domains tested"
76	
77	### 2.3 Claim 降级机制
78	
79	当证据不足以支撑独立 claim 时，应降级为 discussion 中的定性讨论，而非删除。具体做法：
80	- 独立 contribution → Discussion 段落
```

```text
1	---
2	name: paper-writing-norms
3	description: 顶会级别论文写作风格规范——章节写法（Abstract/Intro/Related Work）、去 AI 风格、视觉密度、叙事哲学。Paper Agent 和 Worker 写论文章节时必须加载。
4	allowed-tools: [Read, Write, Edit, Glob, Grep]
5	scope: global
6	version: 2
7	priority: 9
8	tags:
9	  - writing
10	  - paper
11	  - style
12	  - norms
13	roles:
14	  - agent
15	  - worker
16	  - paper_agent
17	---
18	
19	# 论文写作风格规范
20	
21	> 核心哲学：**论文是一个有说服力的故事，不是实验报告。** 审稿人在几十秒内判断论文价值——如果 Abstract 和 Introduction 读起来像实验日志，论文就已经失败了。
22	
23	---
24	
25	## 一、总体写作哲学
26	
27	### 1.1 故事驱动，不是数据堆砌
28	
29	- 论文必须以"故事线"为核心展开：问题 → 洞察 → 方法 → 验证
30	- 禁止大量实验数据堆砌——每个数字出现必须服务于叙事推进
31	- 附录内容必须在正文中有明确引用且融入叙事，禁止简单堆砌
32	- 如果附录数据对核心叙事有重大影响，根据附录数据调整论文 framing
33	
34	### 1.2 主线统一性
35	
36	- 当 idea 在研究过程中发生变化后，**必须重审全文逻辑**
37	- 常见问题：Introduction 讲的故事 A，Method 做的是故事 B，Results 又在讲故事 C
38	- 修复方式：从最终实验结论反推，重写 Introduction 的问题定义和动机
39	- 每次 idea 调整后检查：Abstract → Introduction → Method → Results → Discussion → Conclusion 是否形成一条连贯主线
40	
41	### 1.3 专有名词必须有定义
42	
43	- 首次出现的专有名词/缩写必须给出完整定义和解释
44	- Abstract 中使用的缩写也必须在 Abstract 内定义（如 LLM = Large Language Model）
45	- 不能假设读者知道你自己定义的术语——论文从来没解释过的概念不能直接使用
46	- 通用规则：如果一个非标准术语第一次出现时没有括号定义，就是写作 bug
47	
48	### 1.4 写作前必读同类优秀论文
49	
50	- 每个章节动笔前，找 **2-3 篇目标会议近 1-2 年的高分论文**作为风格参考——同类问题或同类方法皆可
51	- 重点学：
52	  - Abstract 怎么用最少篇幅讲完故事
53	  - Introduction 怎么从大问题切入到具体贡献
54	  - Related Work 怎么 positioning 自己（不是 survey）
55	  - Table/Figure 怎么压缩信息密度
56	- **不是抄袭**，是学习同行的故事节奏和审美——不同子领域叙事节奏差异很大
57	- 实操：Paper Agent 可以从 `literature_map.yaml` 中挑 `role=foundation` 或 `role=direct-competitor` 的论文，把 `{title, venue, year, abstract}` 附在 Worker 的 task_prompt 中作为概念参考；如需学习写作风格本身，让 Worker 通过 WebSearch 取得论文链接并 fetch 全文
58	
59	---
60	
61	## 二、Abstract 写作规范
62	
63	### 2.1 四个必答问题（150-250 words）
64	
65	Abstract 必须在很短的篇幅内清楚回答：
66	1. **研究什么问题** — 1-2 句话交代背景和痛点
67	2. **为什么重要** — 现有方法的不足（不用写太多）
68	3. **本文提出什么** — 核心贡献的关键机制（简要，不堆砌细节）
69	4. **结果说明什么** — 用具体实验结论支撑贡献
70	
71	### 2.2 禁忌
72	
73	- **禁止堆砌数据**：Abstract 不是 Results 的缩写。最多引用 2-3 个关键数字，要服务于 claim
74	- **禁止过度宣传**：不说 "revolutionary"、"groundbreaking"
75	- **禁止写成 Introduction 的缩写**：Abstract 是独立阅读单元
76	- **禁止术语堆积**：如果审稿人几十秒内判断不出价值，就失败了
77	- **缩写必须定义**：Abstract 中首次出现的缩写必须展开
78	
79	### 2.3 审稿人视角检验
80	
```

      "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/paper-writing-norms.skill.md",
      "content": "---\nname: paper-writing-norms\ndescription: 顶会级别论文写作风格规范——章节写法（Abstract/Intro/Related Work）、去 AI 风格、视觉密度、叙事哲学。Paper Agent 和 Worker 写论文章节时必须加载。\nallowed-tools: [Read, Write, Edit, Glob, Grep]\nscope: global\nversion: 2\npriority: 9\ntags:\n  - writing\n  - paper\n  - style\n  - norms\nroles:\n  - agent\n  - worker\n  - paper_agent\n---\n\n# 论文写作风格规范\n\n> 核心哲学：**论文是一个有说服力的故事，不是实验报告。** 审稿人在几十秒内判断论文价值——如果 Abstract 和 Introduction 读起来像实验日志，论文就已经失败了。\n\n---\n\n## 一、总体写作哲学\n\n### 1.1 故事驱动，不是数据堆砌\n\n- 论文必须以\"故事线\"为核心展开：问题 → 洞察 → 方法 → 验证\n- 禁止大量实验数据堆砌——每个数字出现必须服务于叙事推进\n- 附录内容必须在正文中有明确引用且融入叙事，禁止简单堆砌\n- 如果附录数据对核心叙事有重大影响，根据附录数据调整论文 framing\n\n### 1.2 主线统一性\n\n- 当 idea 在研究过程中发生变化后，**必须重审全文逻辑**\n- 常见问题：Introduction 讲的故事 A，Method 做的是故事 B，Results 又在讲故事 C\n- 修复方式：从最终实验结论反推，重写 Introduction 的问题定义和动机\n- 每次 idea 调整后检查：Abstract → Introduction → Method → Results → Discussion → Conclusion 是否形成一条连贯主线\n\n### 1.3 专有名词必须有定义\n\n- 首次出现的专有名词/缩写必须给出完整定义和解释\n- Abstract 中使用的缩写也必须在 Abstract 内定义（如 LLM = Large Language Model）\n- 不能假设读者知道你自己定义的术语——论文从来没解释过的概念不能直接使用\n- 通用规则：如果一个非标准术语第一次出现时没有括号定义，就是写作 bug\n\n### 1.4 写作前必读同类优秀论文\n\n- 每个章节动笔前，找 **2-3 篇目标会议近 1-2 年的高分论文**作为风格参考——同类问题或同类方法皆可\n- 重点学：\n  - Abstract 怎么用最少篇幅讲完故事\n  - Introduction 怎么从大问题切入到具体贡献\n  - Related Work 怎么 positioning 自己（不是 survey）\n  - Table/Figure 怎么压缩信息密度\n- **不是抄袭**，是学习同行的故事节奏和审美——不同子领域叙事节奏差异很大\n- 实操：Paper Agent 可以从 `literature_map.yaml` 中挑 `role=foundation` 或 `role=direct-competitor` 的论文，把 `{title, venue, year, abstract}` 附在 Worker 的 task_prompt 中作为概念参考；如需学习写作风格本身，让 Worker 通过 WebSearch 取得论文链接并 fetch 全文\n\n---\n\n## 二、Abstract 写作规范\n\n### 2.1 四个必答问题（150-250 words）\n\nAbstract 必须在很短的篇幅内清楚回答：\n1. **研究什么问题** — 1-2 句话交代背景和痛点\n2. **为什么重要** — 现有方法的不足（不用写太多）\n3. **本文提出什么** — 核心贡献的关键机制（简要，不堆砌细节）\n4. **结果说明什么** — 用具体实验结论支撑贡献\n\n### 2.2 禁忌\n\n- **禁止堆砌数据**：Abstract 不是 Results 的缩写。最多引用 2-3 个关键数字，要服务于 claim\n- **禁止过度宣传**：不说 \"revolutionary\"、\"groundbreaking\"\n- **禁止写成 Introduction 的缩写**：Abstract 是独立阅读单元\n- **禁止术语堆积**：如果审稿人几十秒内判断不出价值，就失败了\n- **缩写必须定义**：Abstract 中首次出现的缩写必须展开\n\n### 2.3 审稿人视角检验\n",
      "numLines": 80,

          "   page_limit: 8",
          "   deadline: 2026-05-25",
          "   title: >",
          "-    Breaking Symbolic Confirmation Bias: Constraint-Answer Decoupling",
          "-    and Cross-Architecture Verification for LLM Logical Reasoning",
          "+    Beyond Self-Consistency: Why Self-Extracted Constraints",
          "+    Fail to Improve LLM Logical Reasoning",
          "+  title_alternative: >",
          "+    The Symbolic Mirage: Why Formal Self-Verification",
          "+    Adds No Information Beyond Majority Voting",
          "+  title_note: >",
          "+    Alternative title is punchier and highlights the paradox.",
          "+    Director decides. Current title is safe; alternative is higher-impact.",
          "   core_claim: >",
          "-    Symbolic confirmation bias (4.97× bias ratio) causes self-extracted",
          "-    FOL constraints to confirm LLM trace answers regardless of correctness.",
          "-    Two independent cures with different mechanisms: (1) Constraint-Answer",
          "-    Decoupling (G) truncates trace conclusions (4.1% text), cutting the",
          "-    conclusion→FOL causal path (+11.3pp vs SC, +6.4pp vs SICA on 70/204",
          "-    intermediate); (2) Cross-Architecture NLI Verification (R) uses",
          "-    encoder-only DeBERTa-large as architecturally independent verifier",
          "-    (+4.41pp, p=0.039). G=information-flow intervention, R=architectural",
          "-    diversity. Both independently validate the causal model.",
          "+    Provably sound Z3 verification becomes informationally vacuous",
          "+    under self-extraction: MAX-SAT agrees with majority voting on",
          "+    94.4% of problems because self-extracted constraints inherit",
          "+    the model's distributional bias (BR 1.66-4.97x across nine models).",
          "+    Three zero-cost diagnostics classify all 60 tested conditions.",
          "+    Cross-architecture NLI provides preliminary evidence of escaping",
          "+    the bottleneck.",
          "   paper_type: >",
          "-    Dual positive method paper: diagnosis (confirmation bias) + two",
          "-    independent solutions with different mechanisms (G=info-flow, R=arch-diversity)",
          "-  framing: >",

          "-  created_at: 2026-05-19T20:30:00Z",

Mirage Hypothesis 重构**\n- 删除 Finding 1/2 tcolorboxes（后验观察不该用定理框）\n- 删除 O1/O2/P1 标签\n- 在 §5 展示 SCB 数据后自然提出假说 → §6 检验 → 更自然的科学叙事\n\n**5. Power caveat**\n- Abstract 中增加 \"ten conditions underpowered for effects below ~2pp\"\n\n**7. 惊喜度提升：Z3 悖论**\n- 重构 hook：\"Even provably sound formal verification becomes vacuous under self-extraction\"\n- 区分 Huang et al.（NL self-correction fails）vs 我们（FORMAL self-verification fails, with correct solver）\n- 强调：Z3 provably correct + 99% constraints valid + oracle +30pp → yet MAX-SAT = majority voting on 94.4%\n\n### 新章节结构\n\n| 章节 | 页数 | 关键变化 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| §1 Introduction | 1.0 | 更尖锐的 hook，贡献列表 16→10 行 |\n| §2 Related Work | 0.65 | 3 段（合并 CJT），砍 positioning |\n| §3 Method | 0.85 | 删 CJT/Mirage，加 setup |\n| §4 Results | 1.4 | Table 1 提前 + regime scatter |\n| §5 The Symbolic Mirage | 2.0 | 核心分析，展开 remediation，新诊断图 |\n| §6 Cross-Architecture Escape | 1.0 | 软化措辞 |\n| §7 Discussion | 0.6 | 从 2 段扩到 4 段 |\n\n### 新增 Figure 需求\n- **Diagnostic Decision Tree**（TikZ 内联）：C₀ → K_eff → SC → BR 决策流程，标注 60/60 分类准确率。纯文本数据，不需要实验。\n\n### 数据一致性 Flag\n- solutions.tex 仍显示旧值（39.33%/+6.67pp），D152 应更新为 39.17%/+7.00pp。需确认是否 D152 patch 未生效。\n- introduction.tex line 28 同样显示 +6.67pp（旧值）\n\n### Title 建议（可选）\n当前：\"Beyond Self-Consistency: Why Self-Extracted Constraints Fail to Improve LLM Logical Reasoning\"\n备选：\"The Symbolic Mirage: Why Formal Self-Verification Adds No Information Beyond Majority Voting\"\n备选更 punchy，但 Director 决定。\n\n### 执行计划\n9 个 Worker 步骤（串行）+ 一致性检查 + Reviewer。预估 ~12 小时完成。\n\n**需要决策：**\n1. 方案是否批准？\n2. [Director 指令] **补充指令：理论深度强化 — A/B/C/D 四个方向全做**

用户要求在写作重构的同时，加强论文的理论深度和概念强度。**质量优先于速度**。以下四个方向全部纳入你的 writing_plan：

## A. SCB 概率证明（最高优先级）

把 Symbolic Confirmation Bias 从"经验观察"升级为"理论结果"。构建简单概率模型：

- 模型 M 对问题 q 生成答案 a_M
- 从包含 a_M 的 trace 提取约束 c
- 证明 E[BR] ≥ 1：P(c supports a_M | a_M ≠ a*) > P(c supports a* | a_M ≠ a*)

核心洞察：约束 c 是 trace 的有损函数，trace 已经包含 a_M → c 继承 a_M 的分布先验。这是信息论上的必然，不是模型能力问题。

目标：在 §3.3 或 §5.2 中用 1-2 段给出 clean 的形式化论证。不需要长证明，要 elegant。

## B. CIL（Cumulative Information Lift）信息论指标

形式化定义：CIL(V, G) = I(V_output; ground_truth | G_answer)

- Self-extraction → CIL ≈ 0（约束不含超出答案的信息）
- Cross-architecture → CIL > 0（独立模型带来新信息）

这个指标如果能统一解释所有条件的 combo gain 方向，就比 K_eff + BR + C₀ 三个 ad hoc 指标更优雅。**这可能需要 Agent 用已有实验数据计算 mutual information**——如果你确定需要哪些数据，列出来，我让 Agent 准备。

## C. Capability Gap 理论模型

当前 capability gap 只有 n=6 的经验观察。需要一个简单的理论推导：

假设 verifier V（准确率 V_acc）与 generator G（准确率 SC_G）条件独立，推导 combo gain 与 SC_G 的关系。至少要能解释为什么 gain 随 SC_G 单调递减——当 SC_G → 1 时 gain → 0（天花板），当 SC_G 很低时 gain 受 V_acc 上界控制。

## D. 因果 DAG

画一张 M → T → A → C → S 的因果图，用 d-separation 论证 C ⊥ ground_truth | A。这张图直观解释了 self-extraction 失败的信息论根源：**约束的所有信息都经过"答案"这个瓶颈节点**。

可放在 §3.3（方法论基础）或作为 Figure 插入 pages 3-5 的文字墙区域（一举两得：加理论深度 + 打破纯文字）。

## 与写作重构的关系

这四个方向应该融入你的整体重构方案：
- A + D 可能改变 §3.3 Symbolic Mirage 的整个写法（从 ad hoc hypothesis → 有理论支撑的 framework）
- B 可能影响 §5 或 §6 的结构（CIL 作为统一指标替代部分 diagnostics）
- C 强化 §6 的理论基础

**质量要求**：
- 形式化论证要 rigorous 但 accessible——审稿人是 NLP 背景，不是数学家
- 每个理论结果要与已有经验数据对应（理论预测 X，实验验证 X）
- 不要为了加理论而牺牲可读性——如果推导太长，核心结果放正文，完整证明放附录

请把这四个方向整合进 writing_plan.yaml 一起提交审批。如果需要 Agent 计算新数据（比如 CIL），在 plan 中标注清楚。

For self-extraction, CIL should be near zero since the verifier's SICA answers match the generator's SC answers 94.4% of the time, meaning the verifier output adds minimal information beyond what the generator already provided. For cross-architecture with DeBERTa NLI, CIL will be positive because the NLI predictions diverge from the generator's answers in meaningful ways, allowing the verifier to provide genuinely new information about correctness.

Rather than computing mutual information directly from finite samples (which introduces bias), I can use the Conditional Correction Ratio as a practical proxy—it already captures the fraction of generator errors where the verifier provides the correct signal. My plan is to formally define CIL in the methods section, operationalize it through CCR, report the existing CCR values from the appendix, and optionally compute actual mutual information values if time permits, though CCR alone should suffice for the analysis.
````

</details>

```text
200	
201	\section{Capability-Accuracy Constraint Diagnostics}
202	\label{app:diagnostics}
203	\label{app:cil}
204	
205	\paragraph{Conditional Correction Ratio.}
206	We define the \textit{Conditional Correction Ratio} (CCR) to summarize verifier--generator complementarity:
207	\begin{equation}
208	\label{eq:cil}
209	\text{CCR}(V, G) = \log \frac{P(V \text{ correct} \mid G \text{ wrong})}{P(V \text{ correct} \mid G \text{ correct})}.
210	\end{equation}
211	$\text{CCR} > 0$ means $V$ preferentially corrects $G$'s errors; $\text{CCR} < 0$ indicates shared bias. CCR links our findings: (1)~SCB corresponds to $\text{CCR}(\text{self}, G) \approx 0$; (2)~Finding~2 predicts $\text{CCR}(V, G) > 0$ when $V$ is independent and capable.
212	
213	\paragraph{Empirical CCR across verifier--generator pairs.}
214	Table~\ref{tab:cil_empirical} reports CCR for eight verifier--generator--dataset conditions, sorted by CCR descending. All three cross-architecture NLI pairs on ProofWriter yield $\text{CCR} > 0$ (verifier preferentially corrects generator errors), while all three same-model SICA conditions yield $\text{CCR} < 0$ (shared bias). CCR correlates with combo gain: Spearman $\rho = 0.839$, 95\% bootstrap CI $[0.338, 0.990]$; LOO-robust (min $p = 0.014$). Four of eight conditions share the DeBERTa verifier, yielding effective $n < 10$; results should be interpreted as a consistent pattern rather than a precise effect size.
215

Good, I now see the CCR data. The CCR is already defined and there's data for 8 conditions. The correlation with combo gain is ρ = 0.839.

**A. SCB Probabilistic Proof**

The proof is actually simple and elegant:

Proposition: Under same-trace self-extraction, E[BR] ≥ 1 whenever the model errs.

Proof sketch:
- For question q, let K traces produce answers {a_1, ..., a_K}
- Let a_M = majority answer. When a_M ≠ a* (model wrong), n_M > K/2 traces support a_M


- Under same-trace coupling, constraints extracted from traces supporting the majority answer have a coupling probability of at least 0.5 (empirically around 0.94)
- This means the expected constraint count for the majority answer scales with n_M, while the expected count for the correct answer scales with n_*, giving us a ratio greater than 1 since n_M exceeds n_*

The elegance here is that the proof reduces to a simple counting argument: majority traces generate more constraints than minority traces, and the coupling mechanism preserves this ratio.

When majority voting picks the wrong answer, the shuffle control confirms BR_null ≈ BR_obs because the bias is purely structural—it follows necessarily from having more traces supporting the incorrect answer. The formal solver can't resolve this because it only validates individual constraint syntax, missing the distributional skew that emerges across the entire constraint set.

Now I'm formalizing this insight: defining the setup with K traces and their answers, stating the same-trace coupling assumption (which holds empirically about 94% of the time), and then proving that BR must exceed 1 whenever the model errs under this coupling. The shuffle control validates this result empirically. Now I'm mapping out the causal structure: the question generates both the reasoning trace and ground truth, the trace produces the answer, and the answer determines which constraints get extracted and ultimately the aggregation score. The key insight is that constraints and ground truth are conditionally independent given the answer—the only path between them runs through the answer itself, so conditioning on it blocks any information flow, meaning constraints add nothing beyond what the answer already provides.

For cross-architecture evaluation, the structure shifts: now the constraints come from an independent verification source rather than the model's trace, creating a separate information pathway to the score that doesn't go through the model's answer. This independent path is what allows constraints to contribute genuinely new information, which is why we see positive CIL values. I should visualize both scenarios side by side in a figure—the self-extraction case showing how the answer blocks the constraint-to-ground-truth path, and the cross-architecture case highlighting that independent verification channel.

Now I'm formalizing this with conditional mutual information: CIL measures how much additional information the verifier provides about the ground truth beyond what the generator's answer already tells us. In self-extraction this collapses to nearly zero due to the d-separation, but cross-architecture preserves it. The practical metric we already have—the correction rate—operationalizes this same concept, so I should connect the theory to what we're actually measuring in experiments.

论文已有 CCR 定义和数据（Appendix D），这为 CIL 理论框架提供了现成的经验基础。

现在推导各理论方向的核心论证，确保 rigorous 且 compact。

**A. SCB 证明核心推导：**
- 当模型出错时 (a_M ≠ a*)，n_M > n_* (majority 定义)
- Same-trace coupling: 约束 c 支持其 source trace 的答案
- 因此 |C_{a_M}| / |C_{a*}| = n_M / n_* > 1 → BR > 1 是数学必然
- Shuffle control 验证：BR_null ≈ BR_obs (p > 0.7)

**D. 因果 DAG d-separation：**
- DAG: Q → T → A → C → S; Q → GT
- C ⊥ GT | A：条件化 A 后阻断 T → A → C 链
- 含义：约束不含超出答案的 GT 信息 → MAX-SAT ≈ 投票

**B. CIL = I(V; GT | G_answer)：**
- Self-extraction: CIL ≈ 0 (d-separation 的直接推论)
- Cross-architecture: CIL > 0 (V 有独立的 Q → V 信息路径)
- CCR 是 CIL 的实用操作化 (已有 ρ = 0.84, p = 0.014)

**C. Capability gap：**
- ΔCombo ≈ (1-SC_G)·V_acc - SC_G·(1-V_acc) = V_acc - SC_G
- Monotonically decreasing in SC_G, crossover at SC_G ≈ V_acc

更新 writing_plan.yaml 整合四个方向。

      "content": "# Writing Plan — symb_invariant_consensus\n# V4 STRATEGIC RESTRUCTURE: Presentation-first upgrade for 4.0+\n# Target: EMNLP 2026 ARR May cycle, DDL 2026-05-25\n# Updated: 2026-05-23T16:00:00Z\n\nmeta:\n  venue: EMNLP 2026\n  track: main (ARR May cycle)\n  template: docs/paper/acl.sty\n  page_limit: 8\n  deadline: 2026-05-25\n  title: >\n    Beyond Self-Consistency: Why Self-Extracted Constraints\n    Fail to Improve LLM Logical Reasoning\n  title_alternative: >\n    The Symbolic Mirage: Why Formal Self-Verification\n    Adds No Information Beyond Majority Voting\n  title_note: >\n    Alternative title is punchier and highlights the paradox.\n    Director decides.   "old_string": "# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n# NEW SECTION STRUCTURE\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────",
  "new_string": "# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n# THEORETICAL DEPTH ENHANCEMENTS (A/B/C/D)\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────\ntheoretical_enhancements:\n\n  A_scb_proof:\n    priority: highest\n    placement: \"§3.2 (The Information Bottleneck)\"\n    space_cost: \"~5 lines + 1 equation = ~0.10 pages\"\n    core_argument: |\n      Proposition (BR ≥ 1 under same-trace coupling):\n      When a_M ≠ a* (model errs), n_M > n_* by definition of majority.\n      Under same-trace coupling (constraint c supports its source trace's\n      answer), |C_{a_M}| ∝ n_M and |C_{a*}| ∝ n_*.\n      Therefore BR = n_M / n_* > 1.\n      This is a mathematical necessity, not an empirical finding.\n    key_equation: >\n      BR = |C_{a_M}| / |C_{a*}| = n_M / n_* > 1\n      (when a_M ≠ a*, by definition of majority voting)\n    validation: >\n      Shuffle control: BR_null ≈ BR_obs (p > 0.7) confirms the bias\n      is structural — proportional to trace-count distribution, not\n      extraction-specific error.\n    elegance_note: >\n      The proof is 3 lines. Core insight: constraints are a lossy function\n      of traces that already contain the answer. BR > 1 follows from the\n      definition of majority voting. No assumptions about model capability.\n\n  B_cil:\n    priority: high\n    placement: \"§3.2 (definition) + §5/§6 (empirical)\"\n    space_cost: \"~4 lines + 1 equation = ~0.08 pages\"\n    definition: |\n      CIL(V, G) = I(V_output; GT | G_answer)\n      \"How much does the verifier add beyond the generator's answer?\"\n    theoretical_predictions:\n      self_extraction: >\n        CIL ≈ 0. By the causal DAG (D), C ⊥ GT | A, so self-extracted\n        constraints contain no information about ground truth beyond\n        the generator's answer. This is the information-theoretic\n        restatement of SCB.\n      cross_architecture: >\n        CIL > 0 when V has an independent information path to GT\n        (i.e., V does not share M's distributional prior).\n    operationalization: >\n      CIL is the theoretical ideal. We operationalize it through the\n      Conditional Correction Ratio (CCR; Appendix D):\n      CCR(V,G) = log[P(V correct | G wrong) / P(V correct | G correct)]\n      CCR > 0 ↔ CIL > 0 (verifier preferentially corrects errors).\n      Empirical CCR correlates with combo gain: ρ = 0.84, p = 0.014.\n    data_needs: >\n      No new computation needed. Existing CCR data (8 conditions in\n      Appendix D) serves as the empirical proxy. The d-separation\n      argument gives CIL ≈ 0 for self-extraction theoretically.\n      OPTIONAL: Agent could compute actual MI values from per-problem\n      (G_answer, V_output, GT) tuples in combo data for a cleaner\n      presentation. Flag as nice-to-have, not blocking.\n\n  C_capability_gap:\n    priority: medium\n    placement: \"§6.1 (before empirical results)\"\n    space_cost: \"~5 lines + 1 equation = ~0.10 pages\"\n    derivation: |\n      Under conditional independence between G (accuracy SC_G) and V\n      (accuracy V_acc):\n      ΔCombo ≈ (1 - SC_G) · V_acc - SC_G · (1 - V_acc)\n             = V_acc - SC_G\n      Properties:\n      - Monotonically decreasing in SC_G ✓\n      - ΔCombo = 0 when SC_G = V_acc (breakeven at capability parity) ✓\n      - ΔCombo > 0 when SC_G < V_acc (verifier stronger) ✓\n      - As SC_G → 1, gain → 0 (ceiling: few errors to correct) ✓\n    empirical_validation: >\n      DeBERTa standalone V_acc = 55%. Model predicts breakeven at\n      SC_G ≈ 55%.
